03662:     /// Clear route geometry on ESP32.
03663:     func clearRouteGeometry() {
03664:         if let acknowledged = sendAcknowledgedNavigationClear() {
03665:             if !acknowledged {
03666:                 recoverFromRideApplicationFailure(
03667:                     "critical navigation clear admission failed",
03668:                     failureReason: .criticalAdmissionFailed
03669:                 )
03670:             }
03671:             return
03672:         }
03673:         _ = sendRouteGeometry(Data())
03674:     }

03676:     /// Returns nil when the negotiated peer or cached GATT table cannot use
03677:     /// the versioned clear contract, preserving the legacy clear fallback.
03678:     private func sendAcknowledgedNavigationClear() -> Bool? {
03679:         guard supportsRideDeliveryAcknowledgement else { return nil }
03680:         guard isConnected,
03681:               isNavigationReady,
03682:               let peripheral = connectedPeripheral,
03683:               let routeCharacteristic = routeGeometryCharacteristic,
03684:               let navigationCharacteristic,
03685:               let routeWriteType = preferredWriteType(
03686:                 for: routeCharacteristic
03687:               ),
03688:               let navigationWriteType = preferredWriteType(
03689:                 for: navigationCharacteristic
03690:               ) else {
03691:             log("Ride acknowledgement capability present without clear characteristics; using legacy clear")
03692:             return nil
03693:         }
03694: 
03695:         // A clear is a state boundary. Obsolete route or maneuver snapshots
03696:         // must not remain behind it and recreate the state after firmware has
03697:         // acknowledged the clear.
03698:         navigationWriteQueue.removePendingWrites(ofClass: .route)
03699:         navigationWriteQueue.removePendingWrites(ofClass: .navigationSnapshot)
03700:         let payloads = [Data(), Data("1|0|Navigation idle".utf8)]
03701:         let characteristics = [routeCharacteristic, navigationCharacteristic]
03702:         let writeTypes = [routeWriteType, navigationWriteType]
03703:         return enqueueAcknowledgedRideApplicationGroup(
03704:             commandType: .navigationClear,
03705:             payloads: payloads,
03706:             completionHandlers: payloads.map { _ in {} },
03707:             dropHandlers: payloads.map { _ in {} },
03708:             failureHandlers: payloads.map { _ in {} }
03709:         ) { [weak self, weak peripheral]
03710:             index, payload, commandID, onDispatch, onDropped, onFailure in
03711:             guard let self, let peripheral,
03712:                   characteristics.indices.contains(index),
03713:                   let characteristic = characteristics[index] as CBCharacteristic?,
03714:                   writeTypes.indices.contains(index) else { return nil }
03715:             let writeType = writeTypes[index]
03716:             let expectsWriteResponse = writeType == .withResponse
03717:             guard payload.count + AuthenticatedBLEWriteSession.frameOverhead <=
03718:                     peripheral.maximumWriteValueLength(for: writeType) else {
03719:                 return nil
03720:             }
03721:             return NavigationWrite(
03722:                 data: payload,
03723:                 label: "acknowledged navigation clear \(index + 1)/2",
03724:                 transportWrite: { [weak self, weak peripheral, weak characteristic] data in
03725:                     guard let self, let peripheral, let characteristic else {
03726:                         return
03727:                     }
03728:                     self.writeDeviceData(
--- end context ---
03878:                 ) else {
03879:                     log("GPS position not queued: write queue unavailable")
03880:                     return false
03881:                 }
03882:                 if let heading {
03883:                     log(String(format: "Queued native GPS position: heading=%.0f", heading))
03884:                 } else {
03885:                     log("Queued native GPS position: heading=invalid")

04851:         (id >= DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationMinPolygonSizeSettingID &&
04852:             id <= DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationPositionMarkerScaleSettingID) ||
04853:             (id >= DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationLabelDensitySettingID &&
04854:                 id <= DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationLabelOrientationSettingID)
04855:     }

04859:             id <= DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationLabelOrientationSettingID
04860:     }

05180:     @discardableResult
05181:     func playDeviceSound(_ sound: DeviceSound, volumePercent: Double) -> Bool {
05182:         guard supportsDeviceSounds else {
05183:             log("Cannot play device sound: connected device does not advertise sound support")
05184:             return false
05185:         }
05186: 
05187:         let packet = sound.playPacket(volumePercent: volumePercent)
05188:         let volume = packet.last ?? UInt8(DeviceSound.defaultVolumePercent)
05189: 
05190:         let label = "sound \(sound.rawValue) at \(volume)%"
05191:         return DevicePacketRouting.sendPreferredThenFallback(
05192:             preferred: { sendNativeMapTransferPacket(packet, label: label) },
05193:             fallback: { sendFallbackMapPacket(packet, label: label) }
05194:         )
05195:     }

05202:     @discardableResult
05203:     func sendPowerButtonHonkConfiguration() -> Bool {
05204:         guard supportsPowerButtonHonk else {
05205:             log("Cannot configure PWR honk: connected device does not advertise support")
05206:             return false
05207:         }
05208: 
05209:         let requestID: UInt32?
05210:         if supportsPowerButtonHonkAcknowledgement {
05211:             requestID = nextPowerButtonHonkRequestID
05212:             nextPowerButtonHonkRequestID &+= 1
05213:             if nextPowerButtonHonkRequestID == 0 {
05214:                 nextPowerButtonHonkRequestID = 1
05215:             }
05216:         } else {
05217:             requestID = nil
05218:         }
05219:         let packet = selectedDeviceSound.powerButtonHonkPacket(
05220:             enabled: isPowerButtonHonkEnabled,
05221:             volumePercent: deviceSoundVolumePercent,
05222:             requestID: requestID
05223:         )
05224:         powerButtonHonkConfigurationError = nil
05225:         clearPendingPowerButtonHonkConfiguration()
05226:         guard supportsPowerButtonHonkAcknowledgement else {
05227:             let sent = routePowerButtonHonkConfiguration(packet)
05228:             if !sent {
05229:                 reportPowerButtonHonkConfigurationFailure(
05230:                     "PWR honk configuration could not be sent"
05231:                 )
05232:             }
05233:             return sent
05234:         }
05235: 
05236:         pendingPowerButtonHonkPacket = packet
05237:         powerButtonHonkAttempt = 0
05238:         let sent = transmitPowerButtonHonkConfiguration(packet)
05239:         if !sent {
05240:             reportPowerButtonHonkConfigurationFailure(
05241:                 "PWR honk configuration could not be sent"
05242:             )
05243:         }
05244:         return sent
05245:     }

05263:             packet,

05266:                 guard let self, self.pendingPowerButtonHonkPacket == packet else { return }
05267:                 self.schedulePowerButtonHonkRetry(

05237:         powerButtonHonkAttempt = 0
05238:         let sent = transmitPowerButtonHonkConfiguration(packet)
05239:         if !sent {

05241:                 "PWR honk configuration could not be sent"
05242:             )
05243:         }

05252:     private func transmitPowerButtonHonkConfiguration(_ packet: Data) -> Bool {
05253:         let label = powerButtonHonkConfigurationLabel(packet)

05255:             schedulePowerButtonHonkRetry(
05256:                 for: packet,
05257:                 after: powerButtonHonkAckTimeout

05259:             return true
05260:         }

05273:                 guard let self, self.pendingPowerButtonHonkPacket == packet else { return }
05274:                 self.reportPowerButtonHonkConfigurationFailure(
05275:                     "PWR honk configuration was dropped before it could be sent"
05276:                 )
05277:             }
05278:         )

05289:     private func powerButtonHonkConfigurationLabel(_ packet: Data) -> String {
05290:         let enabled = packet.count >= 3 && packet[packet.count - 3] == 1
05291:         return "PWR honk \(enabled ? "enabled" : "disabled")"
05292:     }
05293: 
05294:     private func schedulePowerButtonHonkRetry(
05295:         for packet: Data,
05296:         after delay: TimeInterval
05297:     ) {
05298:         powerButtonHonkRetryWorkItem?.cancel()
05299:         let workItem = DispatchWorkItem { [weak self] in
05300:             guard let self, self.pendingPowerButtonHonkPacket == packet else { return }
05301:             guard PowerButtonHonkRetry.shouldRetry(
05302:                 isNavigationReady: self.isNavigationReady,
05303:                 attempt: self.powerButtonHonkAttempt
05304:             ) else {
05305:                 self.reportPowerButtonHonkConfigurationFailure(
05306:                     "PWR honk configuration was not acknowledged"
05307:                 )
05308:                 return
05309:             }
05310: 
05311:             self.powerButtonHonkAttempt += 1
05312:             if !self.transmitPowerButtonHonkConfiguration(packet) {
05313:                 self.reportPowerButtonHonkConfigurationFailure(
05314:                     "PWR honk configuration retry could not be sent"
05315:                 )
05316:             }
05317:         }
05318:         powerButtonHonkRetryWorkItem = workItem
05319:         powerButtonHonkRetryScheduler(delay, workItem)
05320:     }
05321:

05711:     @discardableResult
05712:     func sendDestinationStatus(
05713:         generation: UInt32,
05714:         token: UInt16,
05715:         status: DeviceDestinationStatusCode,
05716:         message: String
05717:     ) -> Bool {
05718:         destinationStatusSequence &+= 1
05719:         return enqueueDestinationStatus(
05720:             generation: generation,
05721:             token: token,
05722:             status: status,
05723:             message: message,
05724:             sequence: destinationStatusSequence,
05725:             attempt: 0
05726:         )
05727:     }
05728: 
05729:     @discardableResult
05730:     private func enqueueDestinationStatus(
05731:         generation: UInt32,
05732:         token: UInt16,
05733:         status: DeviceDestinationStatusCode,
05734:         message: String,
05735:         sequence: UInt64,
05736:         attempt: Int
05737:     ) -> Bool {
05738:         // A DREQ notification itself proves that the connected firmware knows
05739:         // DNST. Allow the reply during the brief post-authentication window
05740:         // before the CAPS response arrives, when the device may still show its
05741:         // retained catalog from the previous connection.
05742:         guard let endpoint = navigationWriteEndpoint,
05743:               isConnected,
--- end context ---
07454:                 coalescingKey: coalescingKey
07455:             )
07456:         }
07457:         let didEnqueue: Bool
07458:         if prioritized, writes.count == 1, coalescingKey != nil {
07459:             didEnqueue = navigationWriteQueue.enqueueCoalescing(
07460:                 writes[0],
07461:                 prioritized: true
07462:             )
07463:         } else if prioritized {
07464:             didEnqueue = navigationWriteQueue.enqueuePrioritizedAtomically(writes)
07465:         } else {
07466:             didEnqueue = navigationWriteQueue.enqueueAtomically(writes)
07467:         }
07468:         guard didEnqueue else {
07469:             log("Destination frames not queued: insufficient write queue capacity")
07470:             return false
07471:         }
07472:         flushPendingNavigationWrites(endpoint: endpoint)
07473:         scheduleNavigationFlushRetryIfNeeded()
07474:         log("Queued \(frames.count) \(label) frame(s)")
07475:         return true
07476:     }

07478:     @discardableResult
07479:     private func sendFallbackMapPacket(
07480:         _ data: Data,
07481:         label: String,
07482:         writeClass: NavigationWriteClass = .settingsControl,
07483:         coalescingKey: String? = nil,
07484:         prioritized: Bool = false,
07485:         atomically: Bool = false,
07486:         payloadProvider: (() -> Data)? = nil,
07487:         onWrite: (() -> Void)? = nil,
07488:         onDrop: (() -> Void)? = nil,
07489:         onWriteFailure: (() -> Void)? = nil
07490:     ) -> Bool {
07491:         guard let endpoint = navigationWriteEndpoint,
07492:               isConnected,
07493:               isNavigationReady else {
07494:             log("Cannot send fallback \(label): navigation endpoint not ready")
07495:             return false
07496:         }
07497: 
07498:         guard data.count <= endpoint.maximumWriteLength else {
07499:             log("Fallback \(label) not queued: \(data.count)-byte packet exceeds \(endpoint.maximumWriteLength)-byte endpoint")
07500:             return false
07501:         }
07502: 
07503:         guard enqueueNavigationWrite(
07504:             data,
07505:             endpoint: endpoint,
07506:             label: "fallback \(label)",
07507:             writeClass: writeClass,
07508:             coalescingKey: coalescingKey,
07509:             prioritized: prioritized,
07510:             atomically: atomically,
07511:             transportWrite: payloadProvider.map { provider in
07512:                 { _ in endpoint.write(provider()) }
07513:             },
07514:             onWrite: onWrite,
07515:             onDrop: onDrop,
07516:             onWriteFailure: onWriteFailure
07517:         ) else {
07518:             log("Fallback \(label) not queued: write queue unavailable")
07519:             return false
07520:         }
07521:         log("Queued fallback \(label): \(data.count) bytes")
07522:         return true
07523:     }

07556:     @discardableResult
07557:     private func sendNativeMapTransferPacket(
07558:         _ data: Data,
07559:         label: String,
07560:         writeClass: NavigationWriteClass = .settingsControl,
07561:         coalescingKey: String? = nil,
07562:         prioritized: Bool = false,
07563:         onDrop: (() -> Void)? = nil,
07564:         onWriteFailure: (() -> Void)? = nil
07565:     ) -> Bool {
07566:         guard isConnected,
07567:               isNavigationReady,
07568:               let peripheral = connectedPeripheral,
07569:               let characteristic = settingsCharacteristic,
07570:               let endpoint = navigationWriteEndpoint,
07571:               let writeType = preferredWriteType(for: characteristic) else {
07572:             return false
07573:         }
07574: 
07575:         let protectedLength = data.count + (authenticatedWriteSession == nil
07576:             ? 0
07577:             : AuthenticatedBLEWriteSession.frameOverhead)
07578:         guard data.count <= endpoint.maximumWriteLength,
07579:               protectedLength <= peripheral.maximumWriteValueLength(
07580:                 for: writeType
07581:               ) else { return false }
07582:         let expectsWriteResponse = writeType == .withResponse
07583: 
07584:         guard enqueueNavigationWrite(
07585:             data,
07586:             endpoint: endpoint,
07587:             label: "native \(label)",
07588:             writeClass: writeClass,
07589:             coalescingKey: coalescingKey,
07590:             prioritized: prioritized,
07591:             transportWrite: { [weak self, weak peripheral, weak characteristic] payload in
07592:                 guard let self, let peripheral, let characteristic else { return }
07593:                 self.writeDeviceData(
07594:                     payload,
07595:                     to: characteristic,
07596:                     on: peripheral,
07597:                     type: writeType
07598:                 )
07599:             },
07600:             transportCanSend: { [weak self, weak peripheral] in
07601:                 guard let self, let peripheral else { return false }
07602:                 return expectsWriteResponse
07603:                     ? !self.writeWithResponseInFlight
07604:                     : peripheral.canSendWriteWithoutResponse
07605:             },
07606:             transportExpectsWriteResponse: expectsWriteResponse,
07607:             transportCharacteristicUUIDString:
07608:                 characteristic.uuid.uuidString,
07609:             onDrop: onDrop,
07610:             onWriteFailure: onWriteFailure
07611:         ) else { return false }
07612:         log("Queued native \(label): \(data.count) bytes")
07613:         return true
07614:     }

09374:     private func handleRideApplicationAcknowledgement(
09375:         _ acknowledgement: RideBLEApplicationAcknowledgementV1
09376:     ) {
09377:         guard let pending = pendingRideApplicationDeliveries[
09378:             acknowledgement.commandID
09379:         ] else {
09380:             // A duplicated or delayed acknowledgement may arrive after the
09381:             // logical command completed. Protected notification sequencing and
09382:             // the command ID make it safe to ignore.
09383:             return
09384:         }
09385:         guard pending.connectionGeneration == rideDeliveryConnectionGeneration else {
09386:             return
09387:         }
09388:         let disposition = RideBLEApplicationAcknowledgementPolicyV1.disposition(
09389:             pending: .init(
09390:                 commandType: pending.commandType,
09391:                 commandID: pending.commandID,
09392:                 stateGeneration: pending.stateGeneration
09393:             ),
09394:             acknowledgement: acknowledgement
09395:         )
09396:         switch disposition {
09397:         case .ignored:
09398:             return
09399:         case .invalidLeaseGeneration:
09400:             finishRideApplicationDelivery(
09401:                 commandID: acknowledgement.commandID,
09402:                 outcome: .transportFailure
09403:             )
09404:             recoverFromRideApplicationFailure(
09405:                 "invalid lease generation in application acknowledgement"
09406:             )
09407:             return
09408:         case .completed(let result):
09409:             let ackStartedAt = pending.physicallyCompletedAtUptime ??
09410:                 pending.admittedAtUptime
09411:             let acknowledgementLatencyMs = max(
09412:                 0,
09413:                 Int(((ProcessInfo.processInfo.systemUptime - ackStartedAt) *
09414:                     1_000).rounded())
09415:             )
09416:             // CoreBluetooth write callbacks do not include a write identity.
09417:             // Keep an outstanding ATT slot occupied until its own callback (or
09418:             // watchdog) arrives so a delayed callback cannot complete a later
09419:             // write. The application ACK still completes the logical command.
09420:             let isAwaitingATTCallback =
09421:                 pendingATTWrite?.applicationCommandID ==

04810:     private func sendPhoneBatteryStatus(force: Bool = false) {
04811:         guard isConnected,
04812:               isNavigationReady,
04813:               hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities,
04814:               supportsBatteryStatusScreen,
04815:               let percentage = DeviceBLEProtocol.phoneBatteryPercentage(
04816:                 from: UIDevice.current.batteryLevel
04817:               ) else {
04818:             return
04819:         }
04820: 
04821:         if (force || percentage != lastSentPhoneBatteryPercent),
04822:            sendSettingPacket(
04823:             id: DeviceBLEProtocol.phoneBatteryLevelSettingID,
04824:             value: percentage,
04825:             label: "phone battery level"
04826:            ) {
04827:             lastSentPhoneBatteryPercent = percentage
04828:         }
04829: 
04830:         let isCharging = UIDevice.current.batteryState == .charging
04831:         if (force || isCharging != lastSentPhoneBatteryCharging),
04832:            sendSettingPacket(
04833:             id: DeviceBLEProtocol.phoneBatteryChargingSettingID,
04834:             value: DeviceBLEProtocol.phoneBatteryChargingValue(
04835:                 isCharging: isCharging
04836:             ),
04837:             label: "phone charging state"
04838:            ) {
04839:             lastSentPhoneBatteryCharging = isCharging
04840:         }
04841:     }

04996:         cancelPendingRideApplicationDeliveries(notifyFailure: true)
04997:         rendererDiagnosticsChunks.reset()
04998:         rendererDiagnosticsSnapshotJSON = nil
04999:         rendererDiagnosticsStatus = "unsupported"
05000:         updateWorkoutTelemetryCapability(false)
05001:         nextDestinationCatalogTransferID = 1
05002:         hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities = true
05003:         _ = reduceRideTransport(.capabilitiesAccepted(
05004:             generation: rideTransportStateMachine.generation,
05005:             schemaVersion: 1
05006:         ))
05007:         log("Device capabilities unavailable; using baseline feature visibility")
05008:         sendScreenSettingsAfterCapabilityNegotiation()
05009:         sendMapProfilesAfterCapabilityNegotiation()
05010:     }
05011:     
05012:     /// Send feature visibility bitmask
05013:     func sendVisibilityMask() {
05014:         sendVisibilityMask(for: .map, synchronizeLegacyProfile: false)
05015:     }
05016: 
05017:     func sendVisibilityMask(for screen: DeviceScreen,
05018:                             synchronizeLegacyProfile: Bool = true) {
05019:         var mask: Int32 = 0
05020:         let settingID: UInt8
05021: 
05022:         switch screen {
05023:         case .map:
05024:             if showBuildings { mask |= (1 << 0) }
05025:             if showGreenSpace { mask |= (1 << 1) }
05026:             if showPaths || (!supportsExtendedMapVisibility && showTracks) { mask |= (1 << 2) }
05027:             if showMajorRoads { mask |= (1 << 3) }
05028:             if showLocalStreets || (!supportsExtendedMapVisibility && showServiceRoads) { mask |= (1 << 4) }
05029:             if showWater { mask |= (1 << 5) }
05030:             if showRailways { mask |= (1 << 6) }
05031:             if showOtherAreas { mask |= (1 << 7) }
05032:             if showRouteOverlay { mask |= (1 << 8) }
05033:             if showCurrentPosition { mask |= (1 << 9) }
05034:             if supportsExtendedMapVisibility {
05035:                 if showServiceRoads { mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.serviceRoadsVisibilityMask }
05036:                 if showTracks { mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.tracksVisibilityMask }
05037:                 mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.extendedVisibilityMarker
05038:             }
05039:             settingID = 8
05040:         case .mapPlusNavigation:
05041:             if mapPlusNavigationShowBuildings { mask |= (1 << 0) }
05042:             if mapPlusNavigationShowGreenSpace { mask |= (1 << 1) }
05043:             if mapPlusNavigationShowPaths || (!supportsExtendedMapVisibility && mapPlusNavigationShowTracks) { mask |= (1 << 2) }
05044:             if mapPlusNavigationShowMajorRoads { mask |= (1 << 3) }
05045:             if mapPlusNavigationShowLocalStreets || (!supportsExtendedMapVisibility && mapPlusNavigationShowServiceRoads) { mask |= (1 << 4) }
05046:             if mapPlusNavigationShowWater { mask |= (1 << 5) }
05047:             if mapPlusNavigationShowRailways { mask |= (1 << 6) }
05048:             if mapPlusNavigationShowOtherAreas { mask |= (1 << 7) }
05049:             if supportsExtendedMapVisibility {
05050:                 if mapPlusNavigationShowServiceRoads { mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.serviceRoadsVisibilityMask }
05051:                 if mapPlusNavigationShowTracks { mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.tracksVisibilityMask }
05052:                 mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.extendedVisibilityMarker
05053:             }
05054:             settingID = DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationVisibilityMaskSettingID
--- end context ---
05051:                 if mapPlusNavigationShowTracks { mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.tracksVisibilityMask }
05052:                 mask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.extendedVisibilityMarker
05053:             }
05054:             settingID = DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationVisibilityMaskSettingID
05055:         case .navigation, .rideStats, .batteryStatus:
05056:             return
05057:         }
05058: 
05059:         sendSetting(id: settingID, value: mask,
05060:                     synchronizeLegacyProfile: synchronizeLegacyProfile)
05061:     }
05062: 
05063:     func isDeviceScreenEnabled(_ screen: DeviceScreen) -> Bool {
05064:         effectiveEnabledDeviceScreensMask & screen.bit != 0
05065:     }
05066: 
05067:     func isOnlyEnabledDeviceScreen(_ screen: DeviceScreen) -> Bool {
05068:         guard isDeviceScreenEnabled(screen) else { return false }
05069:         return availableDeviceScreens.filter { isDeviceScreenEnabled($0) }.count == 1
05070:     }
05071: 
05072:     func setDeviceScreen(_ screen: DeviceScreen, enabled: Bool) {
05073:         guard availableDeviceScreens.contains(screen) else { return }
05074: 
05075:         var mask = effectiveEnabledDeviceScreensMask
05076:         if !supportsBatteryStatusScreen {
05077:             mask |= enabledDeviceScreensMask & DeviceScreen.batteryStatus.bit
05078:         }
05079:         if enabled {
05080:             mask |= screen.bit
05081:         } else {
05082:             let candidateMask = mask & ~screen.bit
05083:             guard candidateMask != 0 else { return }
05084:             mask = candidateMask
05085:         }
05086: 
05087:         let oldDefault = defaultDeviceScreen
05088:         enabledDeviceScreensMask = DeviceScreen.normalizedMask(mask)
05089:         defaultDeviceScreen = DeviceScreen.fallbackDefault(
05090:             for: defaultDeviceScreen.rawValue,
05091:             mask: enabledDeviceScreensMask
05092:         )
05093:         sendEnabledDeviceScreensMask()
05094:         if defaultDeviceScreen != oldDefault {
05095:             sendDefaultDeviceScreen()
05096:         }
05097:     }
05098: 
05099:     var enabledDeviceScreens: [DeviceScreen] {
05100:         availableDeviceScreens.filter { isDeviceScreenEnabled($0) }
05101:     }
05102: 
05103:     var availableDeviceScreens: [DeviceScreen] {
05104:         DeviceScreen.displayOrder.filter { supportedDeviceScreensMask & $0.bit != 0 }
05105:     }
05106: 
05107:     var effectiveDefaultDeviceScreen: DeviceScreen {
05108:         DeviceScreen.fallbackDefault(
05109:             for: defaultDeviceScreen.rawValue,
05110:             mask: effectiveEnabledDeviceScreensMask,
05111:             supportedMask: supportedDeviceScreensMask
05112:         )
05113:     }
05114: 
05115:     func sendEnabledDeviceScreensMask() {
05116:         enabledDeviceScreensMask = DeviceScreen.normalizedMask(enabledDeviceScreensMask)
05117:         defaultDeviceScreen = DeviceScreen.fallbackDefault(
05118:             for: defaultDeviceScreen.rawValue,
05119:             mask: enabledDeviceScreensMask
05120:         )
05121:         var outgoingMask = Int32(effectiveEnabledDeviceScreensMask)
05122:         if supportsBatteryStatusScreen {
05123:             outgoingMask |= DeviceBLEProtocol.currentScreenMaskMarker
05124:         }
05125:         sendSetting(id: DeviceBLEProtocol.enabledScreensSettingID,
05126:                     value: outgoingMask)
05127:     }
05128: 
05129:     func sendDefaultDeviceScreen() {
05130:         defaultDeviceScreen = DeviceScreen.fallbackDefault(
05131:             for: defaultDeviceScreen.rawValue,
05132:             mask: enabledDeviceScreensMask
05133:         )
05134:         sendSetting(id: DeviceBLEProtocol.defaultScreenSettingID,
05135:                     value: Int32(effectiveDefaultDeviceScreen.rawValue))
05136:     }
05137: 
05138:     private var supportedDeviceScreensMask: Int {
05139:         guard hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities else { return DeviceScreen.allScreensMask }
05140:         return supportsBatteryStatusScreen
05141:             ? DeviceScreen.allScreensMask
05142:             : DeviceScreen.legacyScreensMask
05143:     }

05152:     private func sendScreenSettingsAfterCapabilityNegotiation() {
05153:         sendAutomaticDisplayOffSettingAfterCapabilityNegotiation()
05154:         guard supportsDeviceSettings,
05155:               hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities,
05156:               !hasSentScreenSettingsForConnection else {
05157:             return
05158:         }

05160:         sendEnabledDeviceScreensMask()

05163:             sendPhoneBatteryStatus(force: true)
05164:         }
05165:     }

05169:               hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities,

05171:               !hasSentAutomaticDisplayOffForConnection else {
05172:             return
05173:         }
05174:         _ = sendSetting(
05175:             id: DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingID,
05176:             value: automaticDisplayOffEnabled ? 1 : 0
05177:         )
05178:     }

05180:     @discardableResult
05181:     func playDeviceSound(_ sound: DeviceSound, volumePercent: Double) -> Bool {
05182:         guard supportsDeviceSounds else {
05183:             log("Cannot play device sound: connected device does not advertise sound support")
05184:             return false
05185:         }
05186: 
05187:         let packet = sound.playPacket(volumePercent: volumePercent)
05188:         let volume = packet.last ?? UInt8(DeviceSound.defaultVolumePercent)
05189: 
05190:         let label = "sound \(sound.rawValue) at \(volume)%"
05191:         return DevicePacketRouting.sendPreferredThenFallback(
05192:             preferred: { sendNativeMapTransferPacket(packet, label: label) },
05193:             fallback: { sendFallbackMapPacket(packet, label: label) }
05194:         )
05195:     }

05230:                     "PWR honk configuration could not be sent"

06104:     private func updateTrustedPeripheralDescription() {
06105:         if let activeDeviceID,
06106:            let active = knownDevices.first(where: { $0.deviceID == activeDeviceID }) {
06107:             trustedPeripheralDescription = "\(active.name) (\(active.shortIdentifier))"
06108:         } else {
06109:             trustedPeripheralDescription = lastConnectedPeripheralIdentifier?.uuidString ?? "none"
06110:         }
06111:     }

06113:     private func log(_ message: String) {
06114:         print(message)
06115: 
06116:         let timestamp = DateFormatter.bleDebugTimestamp.string(from: Date())
06117:         let line = "\(timestamp) \(message)"
06118:         if Thread.isMainThread {
06119:             appendDebugEvent(line)
06120:         } else {
06121:             DispatchQueue.main.async { [weak self] in
06122:                 self?.appendDebugEvent(line)
06123:             }
06124:         }
06125:     }

06127:     @discardableResult
06128:     private func reduceRideTransport(
06129:         _ event: RideBLETransportEventV1
06130:     ) -> RideBLETransportTransitionV1 {
06131:         let transition = rideTransportStateMachine.reduce(event)
06132:         rideTransportPhase = rideTransportStateMachine.phase
06133:         rideTransportFailureReason = rideTransportStateMachine.lastFailure
06134:         diagnosticsRecorder?.record(
06135:             level: transition == .rejectedInvalidTransition
06136:                 ? .warning
06137:                 : .info,
06138:             category: .ble,
06139:             event: "ride_transport_transition",
06140:             fields: [
06141:                 "role": rideTransportStateMachine.role.rawValue,
06142:                 "phase": rideTransportStateMachine.phase.rawValue,
--- end context ---
08996:             // A capability downgrade is an authorization boundary for health
08997:             // telemetry. Frames admitted while bit 7 was present must not
08998:             // survive backpressure and transmit after that boundary changes.
08999:             navigationWriteQueue.removePendingWrites(
09000:                 withCoalescingKey:
09001:                     DeviceBLEProtocol.workoutTelemetryCoreCoalescingKey
09002:             )
09003:             navigationWriteQueue.removePendingWrites(
09004:                 withCoalescingKey:
09005:                     DeviceBLEProtocol.workoutTelemetryExtendedCoalescingKey
09006:             )
09007:         }
09008:         supportsWorkoutTelemetry = supported
09009:     }

09055:     @discardableResult
09056:     func handleDeviceCapabilitiesNotification(_ data: Data) -> Bool {
09057:         guard data.count >= 4,
09058:               let prefix = String(data: data.prefix(4), encoding: .utf8),
09059:               prefix == DeviceBLEProtocol.deviceCapabilitiesPrefix ||
09060:                 prefix == DeviceBLEProtocol.deviceCapabilitiesV2Prefix else {
09061:             return false
09062:         }
09063: 
09064:         let flags: UInt32
09065:         let legacyExtendedFlags: UInt8
09066:         let powerButtonConfig: Data?
09067:         if prefix == DeviceBLEProtocol.deviceCapabilitiesPrefix {
09068:             guard data.count == 5 || data.count == 6 ||
09069:                     data.count == 8 || data.count == 9 else {
09070:                 rejectDeviceCapabilities("Received invalid device capabilities payload")
09071:                 return true
09072:             }
09073:             flags = UInt32(data[4])
09074:             legacyExtendedFlags = data.count == 6
09075:                 ? data[5]
09076:                 : (data.count == 9 ? data[8] : 0)
09077:             powerButtonConfig = data.count >= 8
09078:                 ? data.subdata(in: 5..<8)
09079:                 : nil
09080:         } else {
09081:             guard data.count >= 9, data[4] == 1 else {
09082:                 rejectDeviceCapabilities("Received invalid CAP2 header")
09083:                 return true
09084:             }
09085:             flags = UInt32(data[5]) |
09086:                 (UInt32(data[6]) << 8) |
09087:                 (UInt32(data[7]) << 16) |
09088:                 (UInt32(data[8]) << 24)
09089:             var offset = 9
09090:             var seenTypes = Set<UInt8>()
09091:             var parsedPowerConfig: Data?
09092:             var valid = true
09093:             while offset < data.count {
09094:                 guard offset + 2 <= data.count else {
09095:                     valid = false

00619: enum DeviceScreen: Int, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
00620:     case map = 0
00621:     case navigation = 1
00622:     case rideStats = 2
00623:     case mapPlusNavigation = 3
00624:     case batteryStatus = 4
00625: 
00626:     var id: Int { rawValue }
00627:     var bit: Int { 1 << rawValue }
00628: 
00629:     var title: String {
00630:         switch self {
00631:         case .map:
00632:             return "Map"
00633:         case .navigation:
00634:             return "Navigation"
00635:         case .rideStats:
00636:             return "Ride Stats"
00637:         case .mapPlusNavigation:
00638:             return "Map + Navigation"
00639:         case .batteryStatus:
00640:             return "Battery Status"
00641:         }
00642:     }
00643: 
00644:     static var allScreensMask: Int {
00645:         allCases.reduce(0) { $0 | $1.bit }
00646:     }
00647: 
00648:     static var legacyScreensMask: Int {
00649:         allScreensMask & ~batteryStatus.bit
00650:     }
00651: 
00652:     static var displayOrder: [DeviceScreen] {
00653:         [.mapPlusNavigation, .rideStats, .map, .navigation, .batteryStatus]
00654:     }
00655: 
00656:     static func normalizedMask(_ rawMask: Int) -> Int {
00657:         normalizedMask(rawMask, supportedMask: allScreensMask)
00658:     }
00659: 
00660:     static func normalizedMask(_ rawMask: Int, supportedMask: Int) -> Int {
00661:         let availableMask = supportedMask & allScreensMask
00662:         let mask = rawMask & availableMask
00663:         return mask == 0 ? availableMask : mask
00664:     }
00665: 
00666:     static func fallbackDefault(for rawDefault: Int, mask rawMask: Int) -> DeviceScreen {
00667:         fallbackDefault(for: rawDefault, mask: rawMask, supportedMask: allScreensMask)
00668:     }
00669: 
00670:     static func fallbackDefault(for rawDefault: Int,
00671:                                 mask rawMask: Int,
00672:                                 supportedMask: Int) -> DeviceScreen {
00673:         let mask = normalizedMask(rawMask, supportedMask: supportedMask)
00674:         let candidate = DeviceScreen(rawValue: rawDefault) ?? .mapPlusNavigation
00675:         if mask & candidate.bit != 0 {
00676:             return candidate
00677:         }
00678:         return displayOrder.first { mask & $0.bit != 0 } ?? .mapPlusNavigation
00679:     }
00680: }

05073:         guard availableDeviceScreens.contains(screen) else { return }
05074: 
05075:         var mask = effectiveEnabledDeviceScreensMask
05076:         if !supportsBatteryStatusScreen {
05077:             mask |= enabledDeviceScreensMask & DeviceScreen.batteryStatus.bit
05078:         }
05079:         if enabled {
05080:             mask |= screen.bit
05081:         } else {
05082:             let candidateMask = mask & ~screen.bit
05083:             guard candidateMask != 0 else { return }

05085:         }

05088:         enabledDeviceScreensMask = DeviceScreen.normalizedMask(mask)

05090:             for: defaultDeviceScreen.rawValue,
05091:             mask: enabledDeviceScreensMask
05092:         )

05095:             sendDefaultDeviceScreen()

04662:     /// Persist and send a runtime map setting to ESP32 when supported.
04663:     @discardableResult
04664:     func sendSetting(id: UInt8, value: Int32,
04665:                      synchronizeLegacyProfile: Bool = true) -> Bool {
04666:         if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.brightnessSettingID {
04667:             deviceBrightnessPercent = DeviceBLEProtocol.normalizedBrightnessPercent(
04668:                 Double(value)
04669:             )
04670:         } else if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingID {
04671:             automaticDisplayOffEnabled = value != 0
04672:         }
04673:         if hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities,
04674:            !supportsIndependentMapProfiles,
04675:            !isSendingNegotiatedMapProfiles,
04676:            synchronizeLegacyProfile,
04677:            Self.isLegacyMapProfileSetting(id) {
04678:             synchronizeMapPlusNavigationProfileWithMap(for: id)
04679:         }
04680:         saveSettings()
04681:         if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingID,
04682:            (!hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities || !supportsAutomaticDisplayOff) {
04683:             log("Automatic display-off setting not sent: connected firmware does not advertise support")
04684:             return false
04685:         }
04686:         if Self.isIndependentMapProfileSetting(id),
04687:            (!hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities || !supportsIndependentMapProfiles) {
04688:             log("Independent map setting id=\(id) not sent: connected firmware does not advertise support")
04689:             return false
04690:         }
04691:         if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationBirdsEyeViewSettingID,
04692:             (!hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities || !supportsBirdsEyeMapNavigation) {
04693:             log("Bird's-eye map setting not sent: connected firmware does not advertise support")
04694:             return false
04695:         }
04696:         if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationBirdsEyePerspectiveSettingID,
04697:             (!hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities || !supportsBirdsEyeMapNavigationPerspective) {
04698:             log("Bird's-eye perspective setting not sent: connected firmware does not advertise support")
04699:             return false
04700:         }
04701:         if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigation3DBuildingsSettingID,
04702:             (!hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities || !supports3DBuildings) {
04703:             log("3D buildings setting not sent: connected firmware does not advertise support")
04704:             return false
04705:         }
04706:         if Self.isStreetLabelSetting(id),
04707:            (!hasReceivedDeviceCapabilities || !supportsStreetLabels) {
04708:             log("Street-label setting id=\(id) not sent: connected firmware does not advertise support")
04709:             return false
04710:         }
04711:         let deviceValue: Int32
04712:         if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.brightnessSettingID {
04713:             deviceValue = Int32(deviceBrightnessPercent)
04714:         } else if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingID {
04715:             deviceValue = value == 0 ? 0 : 1
04716:         } else if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationBirdsEyePerspectiveSettingID {
04717:             let maximum = supportsBirdsEyeMapNavigationStrongerPerspective
04718:                 ? MapNavigationBirdsEyePerspective.maximum.rawValue
04719:                 : MapNavigationBirdsEyePerspective.strong.rawValue
04720:             deviceValue = min(max(value, 0), Int32(maximum))
04721:         } else if id == 9 || id == DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationStreetLineWidthSettingID {
04722:             deviceValue = DeviceBLEProtocol.legacyStreetWidthBoost(
04723:                 fromAbsoluteWidth: value
04724:             )
04725:         } else {
04726:             deviceValue = value
04727:         }
04728:         guard sendSettingPacket(id: id, value: deviceValue, label: "setting id=\(id)") else {
04729:             log("Settings characteristic unsupported; saved local setting id=\(id), value=\(value)")
04730:             return false
04731:         }
04732:         if id == DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingID {
04733:             hasSentAutomaticDisplayOffForConnection = true
04734:         }
04735:         return true
04736:     }

04738:     func sendStreetLabelDensity(for screen: DeviceScreen) {
--- end context ---
04739:         let settingID: UInt8
04740:         let enabled: Bool
04741:         let density: Int
04742:         switch screen {
04743:         case .map:
04744:             settingID = DeviceBLEProtocol.mapLabelDensitySettingID
04745:             enabled = mapLabelsEnabled
04746:             density = mapLabelDensity
04747:         case .mapPlusNavigation:
04748:             settingID = DeviceBLEProtocol.mapPlusNavigationLabelDensitySettingID
04749:             enabled = mapPlusNavigationLabelsEnabled
04750:             density = mapPlusNavigationLabelDensity
04751:         case .navigation, .rideStats, .batteryStatus:
04752:             return
04753:         }
04754:         sendSetting(

04762: 
04763:     @discardableResult
04764:     private func sendSettingPacket(id: UInt8, value: Int32, label: String) -> Bool {
04765:         guard isConnected, isNavigationReady else { return false }
04766: 
04767:         var data = Data()
04768:         data.append(id)
04769:         withUnsafeBytes(of: value.littleEndian) { data.append(contentsOf: $0) }
04770: 
04771:         var fallback = Data(DeviceBLEProtocol.settingsFallbackPrefix.utf8)
04772:         fallback.append(data)
04773: 
04774:         let coalescingKey = id == DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingID
04775:             ? DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingCoalescingKey
04776:             : nil
04777:         let onDrop: (() -> Void)? = id == DeviceBLEProtocol.automaticDisplayOffSettingID
04778:             ? { [weak self] in
04779:                 guard let self else { return }
04780:                 self.hasSentAutomaticDisplayOffForConnection = false
04781:                 self.log("Automatic display-off setting was dropped before transmission")
04782:             }
04783:             : nil
04784: 
04785:         return DevicePacketRouting.sendPreferredThenFallback(
04786:             preferred: {
04787:                 sendNativeMapTransferPacket(
04788:                     data,
04789:                     label: label,
04790:                     coalescingKey: coalescingKey,
04791:                     onDrop: onDrop
04792:                 )
04793:             },
04794:             fallback: {
04795:                 guard fallback.count <= (navigationWriteEndpoint?.maximumWriteLength ?? 0) else {
04796:                     log("Cannot send \(label): write limit exceeded")
04797:                     return false
04798:                 }
04799:                 return sendFallbackMapPacket(
04800:                     fallback,
04801:                     label: label,
04802:                     coalescingKey: coalescingKey,
04803:                     onDrop: onDrop
04804:                 )
04805:             }
04806:         )
04807:     }

04822:            sendSettingPacket(

04824:             value: percentage,
04825:             label: "phone battery level"
04826:            ) {
04827:             lastSentPhoneBatteryPercent = percentage
04828:         }
04829: 
04830:         let isCharging = UIDevice.current.batteryState == .charging
04831:         if (force || isCharging != lastSentPhoneBatteryCharging),
04832:            sendSettingPacket(

04834:             value: DeviceBLEProtocol.phoneBatteryChargingValue(
04835:                 isCharging: isCharging

04837:             label: "phone charging state"
04838:            ) {

04835:       return;
04836:     }

04850:       return;

04863:           deliveryMember.payloadLength != sizeof(kNavigationIdle) - 1 ||

04865:                       sizeof(kNavigationIdle) - 1) != 0) {

04867:             deliveryMember, ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Malformed,
04868:             deliveryLeaseGeneration);
04869:         return;
04870:       }

04872:                    deliveryMember.payloadLength);

05030: class MyRouteCharacteristicCallbacks : public NimBLECharacteristicCallbacks {
05031: public:
05032:   void onWrite(NimBLECharacteristic *pChar) override {
05033:     ScopedNimbleCallback callbackScope;
05034:     const std::string frame = pChar->getValue();
05035:     std::string value;
05036:     if (!unwrapOwnerAuthenticatedPayload(
05037:             device_ownership::AuthenticatedChannel::Route, frame, value,
05038:             "route characteristic")) {
05039:       return;
05040:     }
05041:     if (!requireAuthenticated("route geometry")) {
05042:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Route);
05043:       return;
05044:     }
05045:     ride_delivery_protocol::CommandMember deliveryMember{};
05046:     const RideDeliveryDecodeResult deliveryDecode = decodeRideDeliveryPayload(
05047:         value, ride_delivery_protocol::CommandType::NavigationClear,
05048:         deliveryMember);
05049:     if (deliveryDecode == RideDeliveryDecodeResult::Rejected)
05050:       return;
05051:     const bool hasDeliveryMember =
05052:         deliveryDecode == RideDeliveryDecodeResult::Decoded;
05053:     uint32_t deliveryLeaseGeneration = 0;
05054:     if (hasDeliveryMember &&
05055:         !admitRideDeliveryMember(deliveryMember,
05056:                                  deliveryLeaseGeneration)) {
05057:       return;
05058:     }
05059:     if (hasDeliveryMember &&
05060:         ((deliveryMember.memberCount != 1 &&
05061:           deliveryMember.memberCount != 2) ||
05062:          deliveryMember.memberIndex != 0 ||
05063:          deliveryMember.payloadLength != 0)) {
05064:       noteRideDeliveryMember(deliveryMember,
05065:                              ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Malformed,
05066:                              deliveryLeaseGeneration);
05067:       return;
05068:     }
05069: 
05070:     const uint8_t *payload = hasDeliveryMember
05071:                                  ? deliveryMember.payload
05072:                                  : reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(
05073:                                        value.data());
05074:     const size_t payloadLength = hasDeliveryMember
05075:                                      ? deliveryMember.payloadLength
05076:                                      : value.length();
05077:     const bool accepted = queueMapInput(
05078:         PendingMapInputType::Route, payload, payloadLength, "native",
05079:         hasDeliveryMember ? &deliveryMember : nullptr,
05080:         deliveryLeaseGeneration);
05081:     if (hasDeliveryMember && !accepted) {
05082:       noteRideDeliveryMember(
05083:           deliveryMember, ride_delivery_protocol::Result::ResourceRejected,
05084:           deliveryLeaseGeneration);
05085:     }
05086:   }
05087: };

05161:           ride_delivery_protocol::Result deliveryResult =
05162:               ride_delivery_protocol::Result::ResourceRejected;

05164:           case ApplyResult::Applied:
05165:           case ApplyResult::Cleared:

05110: class MyWorkoutTelemetryCharacteristicCallbacks
05111:     : public NimBLECharacteristicCallbacks {
05112: public:
05113:   void onWrite(NimBLECharacteristic *pChar) override {
05114:     ScopedNimbleCallback callbackScope;
05115:     const std::string frame = pChar->getValue();
05116:     workout_telemetry_transport::dispatchAuthenticatedNativeFrame(
05117:         frame,
05118:         [](const std::string &protectedFrame, std::string &payload) {
05119:           return unwrapOwnerAuthenticatedPayload(
05120:               device_ownership::AuthenticatedChannel::Workout,
05121:               protectedFrame, payload, "workout telemetry characteristic");
05122:         },
05123:         [](const uint8_t *payload, std::size_t length) {
05124:           std::string value(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(payload), length);
05125:           ride_delivery_protocol::CommandMember deliveryMember{};
05126:           const RideDeliveryDecodeResult deliveryDecode =
05127:               decodeRideDeliveryPayload(
05128:                   value, ride_delivery_protocol::CommandType::WorkoutState,
05129:                   deliveryMember);
05130:           if (deliveryDecode == RideDeliveryDecodeResult::Rejected)
05131:             return;
05132:           const bool hasDeliveryMember =
05133:               deliveryDecode == RideDeliveryDecodeResult::Decoded;
05134:           uint32_t deliveryLeaseGeneration = 0;
05135:           if (hasDeliveryMember &&
05136:               !admitRideDeliveryMember(deliveryMember,
05137:                                        deliveryLeaseGeneration)) {
05138:             return;
05139:           }
05140:           if (hasDeliveryMember) {
05141:             const bool canonicalMember =
05142:                 deliveryMember.payloadLength > 0 &&
05143:                 deliveryMember.memberCount <= 3 &&
05144:                 deliveryMember.memberIndex < deliveryMember.memberCount &&
05145:                 deliveryMember.payload[0] ==
05146:                     static_cast<uint8_t>(deliveryMember.memberIndex + 1);
05147:             if (!canonicalMember) {
05148:               noteRideDeliveryMember(
05149:                   deliveryMember, ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Malformed,
05150:                   deliveryLeaseGeneration);
05151:               return;
05152:             }
05153:             payload = deliveryMember.payload;
05154:             length = deliveryMember.payloadLength;
05155:           }
05156:           const workout_telemetry::ApplyResult result =
05157:               handleWorkoutTelemetryPayload(payload, length, "native");
05158:           if (!hasDeliveryMember)
05159:             return;
05160:           using ApplyResult = workout_telemetry::ApplyResult;
05161:           ride_delivery_protocol::Result deliveryResult =
05162:               ride_delivery_protocol::Result::ResourceRejected;
05163:           switch (result) {
05164:           case ApplyResult::Applied:
05165:           case ApplyResult::Cleared:
05166:             deliveryResult = ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Success;
05167:             break;
05168:           case ApplyResult::IgnoredToken:
05169:           case ApplyResult::IgnoredPair:
05170:           case ApplyResult::IgnoredStateRegression:
05171:             deliveryResult = ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Stale;
05172:             break;
05173:           case ApplyResult::RejectedUnauthenticated:
05174:             deliveryResult = ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Unauthorized;
05175:             break;
05176:           case ApplyResult::RejectedLength:
05177:           case ApplyResult::RejectedKind:
05178:           case ApplyResult::RejectedState:
05179:           case ApplyResult::RejectedToken:
05180:           case ApplyResult::RejectedFlags:
05181:           case ApplyResult::RejectedMetric:
05182:             deliveryResult = ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Malformed;
05183:             break;
05184:           }
05185:           noteRideDeliveryMember(deliveryMember, deliveryResult,
05186:                                  deliveryLeaseGeneration);
05187:         });
--- end context ---
05169:           case ApplyResult::IgnoredPair:
05170:           case ApplyResult::IgnoredStateRegression:
05171:             deliveryResult = ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Stale;
05172:             break;
05173:           case ApplyResult::RejectedUnauthenticated:
05174:             deliveryResult = ride_delivery_protocol::Result::Unauthorized;
05175:             break;
05176:           case ApplyResult::RejectedLength:
05177:           case ApplyResult::RejectedKind:
05178:           case ApplyResult::RejectedState:
05179:           case ApplyResult::RejectedToken:
05180:           case ApplyResult::RejectedFlags:
05181:           case ApplyResult::RejectedMetric:

05191: class MyRideAutomationCharacteristicCallbacks
05192:     : public NimBLECharacteristicCallbacks {
05193: public:
05194:   void onWrite(NimBLECharacteristic *pChar) override {
05195:     ScopedNimbleCallback callbackScope;
05196:     const std::string frame = pChar->getValue();
05197:     std::string payload;
05198:     if (!unwrapOwnerAuthenticatedPayload(
05199:             device_ownership::AuthenticatedChannel::RideAutomation, frame,
05200:             payload, "ride automation characteristic") ||
05201:         !requireAuthenticated("ride automation"))
05202:       return;
05203:     if (!ride_automation_runtime::ingestTransportFrame(
05204:             reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(payload.data()), payload.size(),
05205:             millis()))
05206:       Serial.println("BLE Ride Automation: rejected native frame");
05207:   }

05210: /**
05211:  * @brief Settings characteristic callback - receives runtime config from iOS
05212:  * app Format: [settingId:1][value:4] = 5 bytes Setting IDs: 1=minPolygonSize,
05213:  * 2=detailLevel, 3=routeLineWidth, 9=streetLineWidth,
05214:  * 10=positionMarkerScale
05215:  */
05216: class MySettingsCharacteristicCallbacks : public NimBLECharacteristicCallbacks {
05217: public:
05218:   void onWrite(NimBLECharacteristic *pChar) override {
05219:     ScopedNimbleCallback callbackScope;
05220:     const std::string frame = pChar->getValue();
05221:     std::string value;
05222:     if (!unwrapOwnerAuthenticatedPayload(
05223:             device_ownership::AuthenticatedChannel::Settings, frame, value,
05224:             "settings characteristic")) {
05225:       return;
05226:     }
05227: 
05228:     if (value.size() == ride_automation_protocol::FALLBACK_PREFIX_SIZE +
05229:                             ride_automation_protocol::FRAME_SIZE &&
05230:         std::memcmp(value.data(), ride_automation_protocol::FALLBACK_PREFIX,
05231:                     ride_automation_protocol::FALLBACK_PREFIX_SIZE) == 0) {
05232:       if (!ride_automation_runtime::ingestTransportFrame(
05233:               reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(value.data()) +
05234:                   ride_automation_protocol::FALLBACK_PREFIX_SIZE,
05235:               ride_automation_protocol::FRAME_SIZE, millis()))
05236:         Serial.println("BLE Ride Automation: rejected fallback frame");
05237:       return;
05238:     }
05239: 
05240:     if (handleDestinationPickerPayload(value, "native destination picker")) {
05241:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Control);
05242:       return;
05243:     }
05244: 
05245:     if (handleRendererDiagnosticsCommand(value,
05246:                                          "native renderer diagnostics")) {
05247:       return;
05248:     }
05249: 
05250:     if (hasPrefix(value, "MTRN")) {
05251:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Transfer);
05252:       if (!requireAuthenticated("native map transfer control")) {
05253:         return;
05254:       }
05255:       handleMapTransferControlPayload((const uint8_t *)value.data() + 4,
05256:                                       value.length() - 4,
05257:                                       mapTransferStatusCharacteristic);
05258:       return;
05259:     }
05260: 
05261:     if (hasPrefix(value, "MSTS")) {
05262:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Transfer);
05263:       if (!requireAuthenticated("native map transfer status")) {
05264:         return;
05265:       }
05266:       queueTransferControl(ble_transfer::Action::None,
05267:                            ble_transfer::NotifyMap);
05268:       return;
--- end context ---
05193: public:
05194:   void onWrite(NimBLECharacteristic *pChar) override {
05195:     ScopedNimbleCallback callbackScope;
05196:     const std::string frame = pChar->getValue();
05197:     std::string payload;
05198:     if (!unwrapOwnerAuthenticatedPayload(
05199:             device_ownership::AuthenticatedChannel::RideAutomation, frame,
05200:             payload, "ride automation characteristic") ||
05201:         !requireAuthenticated("ride automation"))
05202:       return;

05204:             reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(payload.data()), payload.size(),
05205:             millis()))
05206:       Serial.println("BLE Ride Automation: rejected native frame");
05207:   }
05208: };

05212:  * app Format: [settingId:1][value:4] = 5 bytes Setting IDs: 1=minPolygonSize,
05213:  * 2=detailLevel, 3=routeLineWidth, 9=streetLineWidth,
05214:  * 10=positionMarkerScale
05215:  */

05225:       return;
05226:     }
05227:

05246:                                          "native renderer diagnostics")) {
05247:       return;
05248:     }
05249: 
05250:     if (hasPrefix(value, "MTRN")) {
05251:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Transfer);
05252:       if (!requireAuthenticated("native map transfer control")) {
05253:         return;

05256:                                       value.length() - 4,
05257:                                       mapTransferStatusCharacteristic);
05258:       return;
05259:     }
05260: 
05261:     if (hasPrefix(value, "MSTS")) {
05262:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Transfer);
05263:       if (!requireAuthenticated("native map transfer status")) {
05264:         return;
05265:       }
05266:       queueTransferControl(ble_transfer::Action::None,
05267:                            ble_transfer::NotifyMap);
05268:       return;
05269:     }
05270: 
05271:     if (hasPrefix(value, "DTRN")) {
05272:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Transfer);
05273:       if (!requireAuthenticated("native device transfer control")) {
05274:         return;
05275:       }
05276:       handleGenericTransferControlPayload((const uint8_t *)value.data() + 4,
05277:                                           value.length() - 4,
05278:                                           mapTransferStatusCharacteristic);

05286:       return;
05287:     }
05288: 
05289:     if (hasPrefix(value, "DSTS")) {
05290:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Transfer);
05291:       if (!requireAuthenticated("native device transfer status")) {
05292:         return;

05298:

05301:       return;

05314:     }

05321: class MyAuthCharacteristicCallbacks : public NimBLECharacteristicCallbacks {

05323:   void onWrite(NimBLECharacteristic *pChar) override {
05324:     ScopedNimbleCallback callbackScope;
05325:     std::string value = pChar->getValue();

05327:       power_metrics::noteBlePacket(power_metrics::BlePacketClass::Auth);

05338: /**
05339:  * @brief Load all map settings from NVS at startup
05340:  */
05341: static void loadSettingsFromNVS() {
05342:   Preferences prefs;
05343:   prefs.begin("mapSettings", false);
05344: 
05345:   map_profile_persistence::load(prefs, mapRenderSettings.mapStyle,
05346:                                 mapRenderSettings.mapNavigationStyle);
05347:   mapRenderSettings.mapNavigationBirdsEyeEnabled =
05348:       map_profile_persistence::loadBirdsEyeEnabled(prefs);
05349:   mapRenderSettings.mapNavigationBirdsEyePerspective =
05350:       map_profile_persistence::loadBirdsEyePerspective(prefs);
05351:   mapRenderSettings.mapNavigation3DBuildingsEnabled =
05352:       map_profile_persistence::load3DBuildingsEnabled(prefs);
05353:   mapRenderSettings.mapRotationMode = prefs.getUChar("mapRotMode", 0);
05354:   mapRenderSettings.tapToSwitchScreens = prefs.getUChar("tapSwitch", 0);
05355:   uint8_t storedScreenMask =
05356:       prefs.getUChar("screenMask", DEVICE_SCREEN_SUPPORTED_MASK);
05357:   if (!prefs.getBool("batteryScrV1", false)) {
05358:     storedScreenMask |= deviceScreenBit(DEVICE_SCREEN_BATTERY_STATUS);
05359:     prefs.putUChar("screenMask", storedScreenMask);
05360:     prefs.putBool("batteryScrV1", true);
05361:   }
05362:   mapRenderSettings.enabledScreensMask =
05363:       normalizedEnabledScreensMask(storedScreenMask);
05364:   mapRenderSettings.defaultScreen = normalizedDefaultScreen(
05365:       prefs.getUChar("defaultScreen", DEVICE_SCREEN_MAP_PLUS_NAVIGATION),
05366:       mapRenderSettings.enabledScreensMask);
05367:   mapRenderSettings.disconnectedSleepTimeoutSeconds =
05368:       normalizedDisconnectedSleepTimeoutSeconds(
05369:           prefs.getUInt("discSleepSec", 120));
05370:   const uint32_t storedVisibilityMask = prefs.getUInt("visMask", 0x3FF);
05371:   mapRenderSettings.navigationOverlayVisibilityMask =
05372:       storedVisibilityMask & MAP_VISIBILITY_OVERLAY_MASK;
05373: 
05374:   prefs.end();
05375: 
05376:   Serial.printf("BLE: Loaded settings from NVS - minPolySize=%d, "
05377:                 "detailLevel=%d, routeWidth=%d, streetWidth=%d, "
05378:                 "markerScale=%d, navBirdEye=%d, navBirdTilt=%d, tapSwitch=%d, "
05379:                 "screenMask=0x%02X, defaultScreen=%d, discSleepSec=%lu\n",
05380:                 mapRenderSettings.mapStyle.minPolygonSize,
05381:                 mapRenderSettings.mapStyle.detailLevel,
05382:                 mapRenderSettings.mapStyle.routeLineWidth,

00625: static tileName tileForDeviceScreen(uint8_t screen) {
00626:   switch (screen) {

00641: static uint8_t deviceScreenForTile(tileName tile) {
00642:   switch (tile) {
00643:   case NAV:
00644:     return DEVICE_SCREEN_NAVIGATION;
00645:   case RIDESTATS:
00646:     return DEVICE_SCREEN_RIDE_STATS;
00647:   case MAP_GUIDANCE:
00648:     return DEVICE_SCREEN_MAP_PLUS_NAVIGATION;
00649:   case BATTERY_STATUS:
00650:     return DEVICE_SCREEN_BATTERY_STATUS;
00651:   case MAP:
00652:   default:
00653:     return DEVICE_SCREEN_MAP;
00654:   }
00655: }

00657: static bool isScreenEnabled(tileName tile) {
00658:   return (normalizedEnabledScreensMask() &
00659:           deviceScreenBit(deviceScreenForTile(tile))) != 0;
00660: }
00661: 
00662: static uint8_t normalizedDefaultDeviceScreen() {
00663:   const uint8_t mask = normalizedEnabledScreensMask();
00664:   uint8_t defaultScreen = mapRenderSettings.defaultScreen;
00665:   if (defaultScreen > DEVICE_SCREEN_BATTERY_STATUS) {
00666:     defaultScreen = DEVICE_SCREEN_MAP_PLUS_NAVIGATION;
00667:   }
00668:   if (mask & deviceScreenBit(defaultScreen)) {
00669:     return defaultScreen;
00670:   }
00671:   for (uint8_t screen : DEVICE_SCREEN_CYCLE_ORDER) {
00672:     if (mask & deviceScreenBit(screen)) {
00673:       return screen;
00674:     }
00675:   }
00676:   return DEVICE_SCREEN_MAP_PLUS_NAVIGATION;
00677: }
00678: 
00679: static tileName configuredDefaultTile() {
00680:   return tileForDeviceScreen(normalizedDefaultDeviceScreen());
00681: }
00682: 
00683: static tileName nextEnabledTile(tileName current) {
00684:   const uint8_t currentScreen = deviceScreenForTile(current);
00685:   uint8_t currentIndex = 0;
00686:   for (uint8_t index = 0; index < DEVICE_SCREEN_COUNT; index++) {
00687:     if (DEVICE_SCREEN_CYCLE_ORDER[index] == currentScreen) {
00688:       currentIndex = index;
00689:       break;
00690:     }
00691:   }
00692:   for (uint8_t offset = 1; offset <= DEVICE_SCREEN_COUNT; offset++) {
00693:     const uint8_t screen = DEVICE_SCREEN_CYCLE_ORDER[
00694:         (currentIndex + offset) % DEVICE_SCREEN_COUNT];
00695:     tileName candidate = tileForDeviceScreen(screen);
00696:     if (isScreenEnabled(candidate)) {
00697:       return candidate;
00698:     }
00699:   }
00700:   return configuredDefaultTile();
00701: }
00702: 
00703: static bool nextEnabledMapBackedTile(tileName current, tileName &next) {
00704:   const uint8_t currentScreen = deviceScreenForTile(current);
00705:   uint8_t currentIndex = 0;
00706:   for (uint8_t index = 0; index < DEVICE_SCREEN_COUNT; index++) {
00707:     if (DEVICE_SCREEN_CYCLE_ORDER[index] == currentScreen) {
00708:       currentIndex = index;
00709:       break;
00710:     }
00711:   }
00712: 
00713:   for (uint8_t offset = 1; offset <= DEVICE_SCREEN_COUNT; offset++) {
00714:     const uint8_t screen = DEVICE_SCREEN_CYCLE_ORDER[
00715:         (currentIndex + offset) % DEVICE_SCREEN_COUNT];
00716:     const tileName candidate = tileForDeviceScreen(screen);
00717:     if (isScreenEnabled(candidate) && isMapBackedTile(candidate)) {
00718:       next = candidate;
00719:       return true;
00720:     }
00721:   }
00722:   return false;
00723: }

02052:         mapTileTransitionUsedRenderAhead ? 1U : 0U);
